<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/canvas.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;canvas.py -- the index canvas every technique paints into.

  mat[h, w]   material id (pal.MAT_ID), 0 = nothing
  step[h, w]  form class within the material&#x27;s ramp (0 = core/contact dark .. top = highlight)
  band[h, w]  depth band for aerial perspective (0 near .. 3 far)
  z[h, w]     depth of what was painted there (ray distance, m), for object occlusion
Sprites are dicts of arrays {a: alpha bool, mat: int, step: int} with an anchor (ax, ay) in px.
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from pal import MAT_ID


class Canvas:
    def __init__(self, w, h):
        self.w, self.h = w, h
        self.mat = np.zeros((h, w), np.int32)
        self.step = np.zeros((h, w), np.int32)
        self.band = np.zeros((h, w), np.int32)
        self.z = np.full((h, w), np.inf)
        self.tag = np.zeros((h, w), np.int32)     # free per-technique tags (e.g. 1 = canopy, 2 = water)

    def fill(self, mask, mat, step, band=None, z=None, tag=None):
        if isinstance(mat, str):
            mat = MAT_ID[mat]
        self.mat[mask] = mat if np.isscalar(mat) else mat[mask]
        self.step[mask] = step if np.isscalar(step) else step[mask]
        if band is not None:
            self.band[mask] = band if np.isscalar(band) else band[mask]
        if z is not None:
            self.z[mask] = z if np.isscalar(z) else z[mask]
        if tag is not None:
            self.tag[mask] = tag if np.isscalar(tag) else tag[mask]

    def blit(self, spr, x, y, z, ground_t=None, tol=0.0, band=None, tag=None):
        &quot;&quot;&quot;paint sprite with its anchor at screen (x, y); depth z.  Pixels are skipped where the
        terrain (ground_t) is nearer than z - tol (hidden behind a ridge) or an object nearer than
        z already sits (z-buffer).&quot;&quot;&quot;
        a = spr[&quot;a&quot;]; sh, sw = a.shape
        x0 = int(round(x - spr[&quot;ax&quot;])); y0 = int(round(y - spr[&quot;ay&quot;]))
        xa, ya = max(0, x0), max(0, y0); xb, yb = min(self.w, x0 + sw), min(self.h, y0 + sh)
        if xa &gt;= xb or ya &gt;= yb:
            return
        sa = a[ya - y0:yb - y0, xa - x0:xb - x0]
        m = sa &amp; (self.z[ya:yb, xa:xb] &gt; z)
        if ground_t is not None:
            m &amp;= ground_t[ya:yb, xa:xb] &gt; z - tol
        if not m.any():
            return
        sl = (slice(ya, yb), slice(xa, xb))
        smat = spr[&quot;mat&quot;][ya - y0:yb - y0, xa - x0:xb - x0]
        sst = spr[&quot;step&quot;][ya - y0:yb - y0, xa - x0:xb - x0]
        self.mat[sl][m] = smat[m] if np.ndim(smat) else smat
        self.step[sl][m] = sst[m]
        self.z[sl][m] = z
        if band is not None:
            self.band[sl][m] = band
        if tag is not None:
            self.tag[sl][m] = tag
</pre></details><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/tech_canopy.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;tech_canopy.py -- forests from above: the crown-clump canopy stroke and its cast shadows.

What the oblique photos say (measure/photo_stats.json, NOTES.md &#x27;canopy&#x27;):
  * at 20-45 deg down, conifers still read as upright spires: lit tops on the sun side, the shade
    side and the gap below dark.  Crown lit-caps are ~0.8 x the crown spacing; the gaps between
    them are narrow dark seams (spacing / lit radius = 2.3-2.5 in every canopy photo).
  * crowns are elongated vertically on screen (autocorrelation y/x = 1.25-1.5 for conifers).
  * the value range lives in the stroke: lit cap vs gap is 20-45 L* while the squinted mass is flat.
  * isolated trees cast short, flattened pools of shadow (ground foreshortened by sin 30 = 0.5).
  * broadleaf crowns are clumps of clumps: the dominant bright blob is a sub-crown leaf mass
    (~1/5 of the crown), each lit on its sun side.
What Ferrari does (V16 conifers, V08 jungle canopy): conifer = 1-px spire, drooping tiers
lengthening downward, lit dabs on the sun-side tier tips, ~15-20% holes; broadleaf = stacked
scallops, each a small dome with a lit rim arc over a dark body, black between.

Scale classes by crown width in screen px (w):
  w &gt;= 5   CROWN sprite: a tiny shaded cone / clump cluster, then Ferrari marks on top
  2 &lt;= w   SPIRE mark: lit dab over a dark stem, 1-2 px wide, height from the tree height
  w &lt; 2    TEXTURE: the forest is a ground material; vertical lit-dab/dark-stem dashes stippled
           at the measured lit fraction, dash length = projected tree height (grain grows with nearness)
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from pal import MAT_ID
from dither import threshold_map

CON = MAT_ID[&quot;conifer&quot;]; BRD = MAT_ID[&quot;broadleaf&quot;]
_cache = {}


def _screen_sun(sun, cam):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;sun direction in screen space: (sx, sy) with sy down, and the elevation term.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    sx = float(np.dot(sun, cam.right)); sy = -float(np.dot(sun, cam.up))
    return sx, sy


def conifer_sprite(w, h, sun, cam, seed=0, lit_ok=True, ramp_top=5):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a conifer crown w px wide and h px tall (screen), shaded as a tiered cone and quantised into
    the 6-step conifer ramp (0 core | 1 shadow | 2 reflected || 3 halftone | 4 lit | 5 highlight).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;con&quot;, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), round(sun[1], 2), round(sun[2], 2), seed % 6, lit_ok)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed % 6 + 100 * w + 7 * h)
    W = w + 2; Hh = h + 2
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32)
    cx = (W - 1) / 2.0
    tiers = max(1, int(round(h / 4.5)))
    sxs, sys_ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    side = 1 if sxs &gt;= 0 else -1                   # which screen side is lit
    elev = sun[2]
    for yy in range(Hh):
        v = (yy - 1) / max(h - 1, 1)               # 0 at the tip, 1 at the base
        if v &lt; 0 or v &gt; 1.0:
            continue
        tpos = (v * tiers) % 1.0 if tiers &gt; 1 else v   # position inside a tier (0 top .. 1 bottom)
        r = 0.5 * w * (0.12 + 0.88 * v ** 0.9)
        if h &gt;= 8 and v &gt; 0.12:
            r *= 0.72 + 0.34 * tpos                # drooping spurs: each tier widens to its bottom
        for xx in range(W):
            u = (xx - cx) / max(r, 0.5)
            if abs(u) &gt; 1.0 + 1e-6:
                continue
            a[yy, xx] = True
            # normal of the cone at this screen x: across-the-crown angle, tilted down at tier bottoms
            nx = u; nz = 0.45 + (0.25 if tpos &lt; 0.35 else -0.2 * tpos)
            ny = -np.sqrt(max(0.0, 1 - u * u))     # facing the camera
            n = np.array([nx, 0.0, nz]); n = n / (np.linalg.norm(n) + 1e-9)
            ndl = n[0] * abs(sun[0] if abs(sun[0]) &gt; 1e-3 else 0) * side * np.sign(u) if False else None
            # light: horizontal part from the screen-side, vertical part from the sun elevation
            lit = (u * side) * np.hypot(sun[0], sun[1]) * 0.9 + nz * elev * 1.1
            occ = (0.55 + 0.45 * (1 - v)) * (1 - 0.5 * max(0.0, tpos - 0.6))   # lower &amp; tier bottoms darker
            if lit_ok and lit &gt; 0.12:
                s = 3 + (lit &gt; 0.45) + (lit &gt; 0.8 and v &lt; 0.6)
            else:
                s = 0 + (occ &gt; 0.55) + (occ &gt; 0.85 and (u * side) &lt; -0.2)
            st[yy, xx] = s
    # Ferrari marks ----------------------------------------------------------
    if h &gt;= 6:
        # 1-px spire for the top ~15%
        top = max(1, int(0.15 * h))
        for yy in range(1, 1 + top):
            row = np.nonzero(a[yy])[0]
            if len(row) &gt; 1:
                keep = int(round(cx))
                a[yy, :] = False; a[yy, keep] = True
                st[yy, keep] = 4 if lit_ok else 2
    if h &gt;= 8:
        # lit dabs on the sun-side tier tips; dark undersides just below each tier line
        for k in range(1, tiers + 1):
            yb = 1 + int(round(k / tiers * (h - 1))) - 1
            if 1 &lt;= yb &lt; Hh:
                row = np.nonzero(a[yb])[0]
                if len(row):
                    tip = row.max() if side &gt; 0 else row.min()
                    if lit_ok:
                        st[yb, tip] = 5 if rng.random() &lt; 0.7 else 4
                    if yb + 1 &lt; Hh:
                        under = np.nonzero(a[yb + 1])[0]
                        for xx in under:
                            if abs(xx - cx) &lt; 0.5 * len(under) * 0.6 and st[yb + 1, xx] &lt;= 2:
                                st[yb + 1, xx] = 0
        # ~15% holes in the interior (dark core shows through)
        inner = a.copy(); inner[:, 1:] &amp;= a[:, :-1]; inner[:, :-1] &amp;= a[:, 1:]
        holes = inner &amp; (rng.random(a.shape) &lt; 0.15)
        holes[:int(0.3 * Hh)] = False
        st[holes] = 0
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, CON), step=st, ax=cx, ay=Hh - 1.5)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr


def broadleaf_sprite(w, h, sun, cam, seed=0, lit_ok=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a broadleaf crown: a cluster of 3-7 leaf masses, each shaded as a dome, front masses covering
    back ones (the scallop); lit rim arcs toward the sun, black seams between masses.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;brd&quot;, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), round(sun[1], 2), round(sun[2], 2), seed % 8, lit_ok)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed % 8 + 31 * w)
    W = w + 2; Hh = h + 2
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32); zb = np.full((Hh, W), -1e9)
    sxs, _ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    L = np.array([np.sign(sxs) * np.hypot(sun[0], sun[1]) * 0.8, -0.35 * sun[2], sun[2]])   # screen-space light (x right, y down, z toward viewer/up)
    L = L / np.linalg.norm(L)
    n_cl = int(np.clip(round(w / 3.2), 1, 9))
    cx0 = (W - 1) / 2; cy0 = (Hh - 1) / 2
    blobs = []
    for i in range(n_cl):
        ang = rng.random() * 2 * np.pi; rad = rng.random() ** 0.5 * 0.33
        bx = cx0 + np.cos(ang) * rad * w; by = cy0 + np.sin(ang) * rad * h * 0.9
        br = (0.28 + 0.14 * rng.random()) * w if n_cl &gt; 1 else 0.5 * w
        blobs.append((bx, by, br))
    blobs.sort(key=lambda b: b[1])                    # top (far) first: nearer lower masses cover
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:Hh, 0:W]
    for bx, by, br in blobs:
        dx = (xx - bx) / br; dy = (yy - by) / (br * 0.9)
        d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
        inside = d2 &lt;= 1.0
        nz = np.sqrt(np.clip(1 - d2, 0, 1))
        depth = by + nz * br                           # lower &amp; bulging = nearer
        m = inside &amp; (depth &gt; zb)
        ndl = dx * L[0] + dy * L[1] + nz * L[2]
        if lit_ok:
            s = np.where(ndl &gt; 0.62, 5, np.where(ndl &gt; 0.35, 4, np.where(ndl &gt; 0.12, 3,
                         np.where(ndl &gt; -0.15, 2, np.where(ndl &gt; -0.5, 1, 0)))))
        else:
            s = np.where(ndl &gt; 0.2, 2, np.where(ndl &gt; -0.3, 1, 0))
        # the seam: a dark rim where this mass meets a mass behind it
        edge = inside &amp; (d2 &gt; 0.72) &amp; (dy &gt; -0.2)
        s = np.where(edge, np.minimum(s, 1), s)
        a |= m; st[m] = s[m]; zb[m] = depth[m]
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, BRD), step=st, ax=(W - 1) / 2, ay=Hh - 1.5)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr


def spire_mark(w, h, sun, cam, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;, lit_ok=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;2-4 px wide trees: a lit dab on the sun side over a dark stem (the contrast inside the stroke).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;spire&quot;, kind, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), lit_ok)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    mat = CON if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else BRD
    W = max(1, w); Hh = max(2, h)
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32)
    sxs, _ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    side = 1 if sxs &gt;= 0 else -1
    for yy in range(Hh):
        v = yy / max(Hh - 1, 1)
        if kind == &quot;conifer&quot;:
            half = (W - 1) / 2 * min(1.0, 0.25 + v * 1.2)
        else:
            half = (W - 1) / 2 * (0.7 + 0.3 * np.sin(np.pi * min(1, v * 1.1)))
        for xx in range(W):
            if abs(xx - (W - 1) / 2) &lt;= half + 0.01:
                a[yy, xx] = True
                sunside = (xx - (W - 1) / 2) * side &gt;= 0
                if lit_ok and (v &lt; 0.5 or (sunside and v &lt; 0.8)):
                    st[yy, xx] = 4 if (sunside or W == 1) and v &lt; 0.34 else 3
                else:
                    st[yy, xx] = 1 if v &lt; 0.85 else 0
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, mat), step=st, ax=(W - 1) / 2, ay=Hh - 1)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr


# --------------------------------------------------------------------- stands
def scatter_trees(T, density, spacing, rng, kind_field=None, hmean=18.0, jitter=0.45, clump=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;place trees on a jittered grid of `spacing` metres, kept where rng &lt; density(x, y).
    clump: optional field in [0,1] multiplying density (stand structure: gaps and dense groups).
    Returns arrays x, y, z(base), height, crown width, kind (0 conifer / 1 broadleaf).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = T.extent
    xs = np.arange(x0 + spacing / 2, x1, spacing); ys = np.arange(y0 + spacing / 2, y1, spacing)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(xs, ys)
    X = X + (rng.random(X.shape) - 0.5) * 2 * jitter * spacing
    Y = Y + (rng.random(Y.shape) - 0.5) * 2 * jitter * spacing
    X = X.ravel(); Y = Y.ravel()
    d = T.sample(density, X, Y)
    if clump is not None:
        d = d * T.sample(clump, X, Y)
    keep = rng.random(len(X)) &lt; d
    X, Y = X[keep], Y[keep]
    Z = T.height(X, Y)
    kind = np.zeros(len(X), int) if kind_field is None else (rng.random(len(X)) &lt; T.sample(kind_field, X, Y)).astype(int)
    Ht = hmean * (0.7 + 0.6 * rng.random(len(X)))
    Wc = np.where(kind == 0, Ht * (0.28 + 0.08 * rng.random(len(X))), Ht * (0.55 + 0.2 * rng.random(len(X))))
    return dict(x=X, y=Y, z=Z, h=Ht, w=Wc, kind=kind, seed=rng.integers(0, 1 &lt;&lt; 20, len(X)))


def paint_trees(cv, trees, cam, G, sun, sunvis_fn, ground_mat=None, band_fn=None, shadow=True,
                shadow_dark=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;draw every tree back to front.  Sprites by scale class; cast shadows first (on the ground
    pixels already painted), then crowns with terrain occlusion.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    n = len(trees[&quot;x&quot;])
    if n == 0:
        return
    P = np.c_[trees[&quot;x&quot;], trees[&quot;y&quot;], trees[&quot;z&quot;]]
    sx, sy, depth = cam.project(P)
    fp = cam.footprint(depth)
    wpx = trees[&quot;w&quot;] / fp
    hpx = trees[&quot;h&quot;] * np.cos(np.radians(cam.pitch)) / fp
    vis_sun = sunvis_fn(trees[&quot;x&quot;], trees[&quot;y&quot;])
    hor = np.hypot(sun[0], sun[1]) + 1e-9
    # ---- cast shadows on the ground (drawn into the ground layer before any crown)
    if shadow and sun[2] &gt; 0.02:
        L = np.minimum(trees[&quot;h&quot;] * hor / sun[2], trees[&quot;h&quot;] * 6)   # shadow length (m), clamp at grazing sun
        for i in np.argsort(-depth):
            if vis_sun[i] &lt; 0.5 or wpx[i] &lt; 1.2:
                continue
            # shadow = ellipse from the base toward -sun, length L, width = crown width
            cxw = trees[&quot;x&quot;][i] - sun[0] / hor * L[i] * 0.55
            cyw = trees[&quot;y&quot;][i] - sun[1] / hor * L[i] * 0.55
            ex, ey, _ = cam.project(np.array([cxw, cyw, trees[&quot;z&quot;][i]]))
            # axis of the shadow in screen space
            tip = cam.project(np.array([trees[&quot;x&quot;][i] - sun[0] / hor * L[i], trees[&quot;y&quot;][i] - sun[1] / hor * L[i], trees[&quot;z&quot;][i]]))
            ax_ = np.array([tip[0] - sx[i], tip[1] - sy[i]])
            la = np.linalg.norm(ax_) + 1e-6
            ux, uy = ax_ / la
            half_len = max(la * 0.5, wpx[i] * 0.45); half_w = wpx[i] * 0.45
            rr = int(np.ceil(half_len + 2))
            xa, xb = int(ex - rr), int(ex + rr + 1); ya, yb = int(ey - rr), int(ey + rr + 1)
            xa, ya = max(xa, 0), max(ya, 0); xb, yb = min(xb, cv.w), min(yb, cv.h)
            if xa &gt;= xb or ya &gt;= yb:
                continue
            YY, XX = np.mgrid[ya:yb, xa:xb]
            du = (XX - ex) * ux + (YY - ey) * uy; dv = -(XX - ex) * uy + (YY - ey) * ux
            m = (du / half_len) ** 2 + (dv / (half_w + 0.3)) ** 2 &lt;= 1.0
            m &amp;= np.abs(G[&quot;t&quot;][ya:yb, xa:xb] - depth[i]) &lt; trees[&quot;h&quot;][i] * 2 + 3 * fp[i]
            m &amp;= cv.tag[ya:yb, xa:xb] != 1        # not on canopy already painted
            sub = cv.step[ya:yb, xa:xb]
            ns = cv.nsteps_of(cv.mat[ya:yb, xa:xb]) if hasattr(cv, &quot;nsteps_of&quot;) else None
            if shadow_dark:
                cv.step[ya:yb, xa:xb] = np.where(m, np.minimum(sub, 1), sub)
            cv.tag[ya:yb, xa:xb] = np.where(m, 3, cv.tag[ya:yb, xa:xb])
    # ---- crowns, far to near
    for i in np.argsort(-depth):
        w = wpx[i]; h = hpx[i]
        if w &lt; 0.6 and h &lt; 1.5:
            continue
        lit_ok = vis_sun[i] &gt; 0.5
        kind = &quot;conifer&quot; if trees[&quot;kind&quot;][i] == 0 else &quot;broadleaf&quot;
        if w &gt;= 5:
            wi = int(round(w)); hi = int(round(h if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else max(h * 0.55, w * 0.8)))
            spr = conifer_sprite(wi, hi, sun, cam, trees[&quot;seed&quot;][i], lit_ok) if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; \
                else broadleaf_sprite(wi, hi, sun, cam, trees[&quot;seed&quot;][i], lit_ok)
        else:
            wi = int(np.clip(round(w), 1, 4)); hi = int(max(2, round(h if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else max(h * 0.6, w))))
            spr = spire_mark(wi, hi, sun, cam, kind, lit_ok)
        band = band_fn(depth[i]) if band_fn else None
        cv.blit(spr, sx[i], sy[i] + 0.5, depth[i] - 0.01 * (i % 7), ground_t=G[&quot;t&quot;], tol=trees[&quot;w&quot;][i] * 1.5 + fp[i] * 2,
                band=band, tag=1)


def forest_texture(cv, mask, G, cam, sun, sunvis, ndl, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;, tree_h=18.0, crown_w=5.0,
                   lit_frac=0.45, seed=0, band=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;far forest as a ground material.  Each pixel of `mask` is painted as canopy with vertical
    dashes: a lit dab (step 3/4) on top of a dark stem (step 1/0), the dash length the projected tree
    height, stippled with density = lit_frac scaled by the slope&#x27;s light.  Where the sun can&#x27;t reach,
    the whole mass drops to the shadow family with sparse reflected-light dabs.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    mat = CON if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else BRD
    h, w = mask.shape
    fp = G[&quot;mpp&quot;]
    dash = np.clip(np.round(tree_h * np.cos(np.radians(cam.pitch)) / np.maximum(fp, 1e-3)), 1, 4).astype(int)
    thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    thr2 = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed + 7)
    light = np.clip(ndl, 0, 1) * sunvis
    dens = np.clip(lit_frac * (0.35 + 1.1 * light), 0.05, 0.85)
    step = np.where(light &gt; 0.08, 1, 0).astype(np.int32)        # the gap/body tone
    step[(light &gt; 0.08) &amp; (thr2 &lt; 0.25)] = 2
    heads = mask &amp; (thr &lt; dens)
    # a head is lit (3/4) in sun, reflected (2) in shade; the pixels below the head are its stem
    lit = light &gt; 0.08
    headstep = np.where(lit, np.where(light &gt; 0.55, 4, 3), 2)
    out = step.copy()
    yy, xx = np.nonzero(heads)
    for y, x in zip(yy, xx):
        out[y, x] = headstep[y, x]
        L = dash[y, x]
        for k in range(1, L):
            if y + k &lt; h and mask[y + k, x] and not heads[y + k, x]:
                out[y + k, x] = 0 if k == L - 1 else min(out[y + k, x], 1)
    cv.fill(mask, mat, out, band=band, tag=1)

ok</pre></details><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/tech_ground.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;tech_ground.py -- open ground from above: the slope-tone ramp and ground textures.

Slope-tone ramp (the technique for slopes whose silhouettes are gone).  From 30 deg down and
farther, a slope has no outline; it reads by *tone planes*: every facet facing the sun is one flat
light value, every facet turned away is one flat shadow value, and the turn between them is a
narrow dithered seam (Ferrari V14: mountain flanks as flat planes with checker seams; the photos:
sunlit facets vs shaded gully walls of a bare slope differ by 20-35 L* while each facet is flat,
NOTES.md &#x27;slopes&#x27;).  So:
  * the step coordinate comes from n.l (lit family) or sky-view + bounce (shadow family),
  * it is *posterised into planes* (flat_bands with a narrow seam) rather than smoothly dithered,
  * the lit/shadow families never mix: the terminator is a hard edge,
  * fall-line flecks: a sparse 1-px dark/light fleck elongated along the fall line gives the slope
    its direction (grass stipple on meadows is isotropic instead).
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from pal import MATERIALS, MAT_ID
from dither import threshold_map, quantize, flat_bands


def form_coordinate(ndl, sunvis, skyview, nsteps, ao=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;continuous step coordinate in [0, nsteps-1] with a jump at the terminator.
    Shadow family: [0, ns-1] from skyview (and ao).  Lit family: [ns, n-1] from n.l.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    ns = nsteps // 2; nl = nsteps - ns
    lit = (ndl &gt; 0.06) &amp; (sunvis &gt; 0.5)
    sh = np.clip(skyview, 0, 1)
    if ao is not None:
        sh = sh * np.clip(ao, 0, 1)
    xs = sh * (ns - 1 + 0.49)                       # 0 .. ns-1 (+ half, so the top shadow step shows)
    xl = ns + np.clip((ndl - 0.06) / 0.9, 0, 1) * (nl - 1 + 0.49)
    return np.where(lit, xl, xs), lit, ns


def slope_tone(ndl, sunvis, skyview, mat, thr, seam=0.22, ao=None, planes=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;step for every pixel of one material: posterised planes with narrow dither seams, clamped
    into its light family (no dither across the terminator).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    n = MATERIALS[mat][&quot;steps&quot;]
    x, lit, ns = form_coordinate(ndl, sunvis, skyview, n, ao)
    s = flat_bands(x, thr, seam) if planes else quantize(x, thr)
    s = np.where(lit, np.clip(s, ns, n - 1), np.clip(s, 0, ns - 1))
    return s


def fall_line_flecks(step, mask, G, thr, density=0.06, lit_ok=None, seed=0):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;sparse flecks along the fall line: a dark pixel with a lighter pixel uphill of it.
    The fall line on screen is the projected downslope direction.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    h, w = step.shape
    n = G[&quot;n&quot;]
    # downslope direction in world xy is -grad = (n_x, n_y); on an iso screen with yaw 0 world +y
    # is screen up, so screen down-slope is (n_x, -n_y) (sign for rows)
    dx = n[..., 0]; dy = -n[..., 1]
    ln = np.hypot(dx, dy) + 1e-6
    dx /= ln; dy /= ln
    pick = mask &amp; (thr &lt; density)
    yy, xx = np.nonzero(pick)
    out = step.copy()
    for y, x in zip(yy, xx):
        ox = int(round(dx[y, x])); oy = int(round(dy[y, x]))
        y2, x2 = y - oy, x - ox
        out[y, x] = max(0, step[y, x] - 1)
        if 0 &lt;= y2 &lt; h and 0 &lt;= x2 &lt; w and mask[y2, x2]:
            out[y2, x2] = step[y2, x2] + (1 if (lit_ok is None or lit_ok[y2, x2]) else 0)
    return out


def stipple(step, mask, thr, up=0.18, down=0.14, lo=None, hi=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;isotropic grain: a share of pixels one step up, a share one step down (within the family).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    out = step.copy()
    out = np.where(mask &amp; (thr &lt; up), step + 1, out)
    out = np.where(mask &amp; (thr &gt; 1 - down), step - 1, out)
    if lo is not None:
        out = np.maximum(out, lo)
    if hi is not None:
        out = np.minimum(out, hi)
    return out


def family_bounds(mat, lit):
    n = MATERIALS[mat][&quot;steps&quot;]; ns = n // 2
    lo = np.where(lit, ns, 0); hi = np.where(lit, n - 1, ns - 1)
    return lo, hi
</pre></details><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/painter.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;painter.py -- the thin scaffold: G-buffer -&gt; index canvas (via the technique portfolio) -&gt; palette.

A Scene holds what the simulator owns (terrain, cover fields, objects).  paint() only decides how it
looks: it calls one technique per material/object and never invents geography.

  sc = Scene(T, cover={...}, trees=..., huts=[...], fires=[...], water=...)
  img, info = paint(sc, cam, LIGHTS[&#x27;afternoon&#x27;])
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
from pal import Palette, MAT_ID, MATERIALS
from canvas import Canvas
from dither import threshold_map
import tech_ground as TG
import tech_canopy as TC

GROUND_ORDER = [&quot;meadow&quot;, &quot;drygrass&quot;, &quot;soil&quot;, &quot;rock&quot;, &quot;scree&quot;, &quot;sand&quot;, &quot;snow&quot;]


class Scene:
    def __init__(self, T, cover, trees=None, water=None, huts=None, fires=None, extras=None, seed=0):
        self.T = T
        self.cover = cover            # name -&gt; world grid [0,1] (ground materials + &#x27;forest&#x27;)
        self.trees = trees
        self.water = water            # dict(mask=grid bool or level=float, kind=&#x27;river&#x27;|&#x27;lake&#x27;|&#x27;sea&#x27;)
        self.huts = huts or []
        self.fires = fires or []
        self.extras = extras or {}
        self.seed = seed
        self._cache = {}

    def field(self, name, fn):
        if name not in self._cache:
            self._cache[name] = fn()
        return self._cache[name]


def band_of(t, bands):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;depth band index from ray distance (m) and band edges.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    b = np.zeros(np.shape(t), np.int32)
    for i, e in enumerate(bands):
        b = np.where(t &gt; e, i + 1, b)
    return b


def ground_materials(sc, G, thr):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;pick one ground material per pixel from the cover weights; boundaries get a 1-2 px fringe
    (texture ends at a mass boundary, it isn&#x27;t outlined).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    names = [n for n in GROUND_ORDER if n in sc.cover]
    Wt = np.stack([sc.T.sample(sc.cover[n], G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;]) for n in names], 0)
    Wt = Wt + (thr[None] - 0.5) * 0.18                 # fringe
    idx = np.argmax(Wt, 0)
    return names, idx, Wt


def paint(sc, cam, light, bands=None, forest_mode=&quot;auto&quot;, tex_threshold=2.0, return_canvas=False):
    T = sc.T
    G = T.gbuffer(cam)
    h, w = G[&quot;hit&quot;].shape
    cv = Canvas(w, h)
    sun = light.vec
    seed = sc.seed
    thr_b = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    thr_4 = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;bayer4&quot;)
    shadow = sc.field((&quot;shadow&quot;, light.name), lambda: T.sun_shadow(sun, soft=T.cell * 0.5))
    sunvis = T.sample(shadow, G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;])
    ndl = np.clip((G[&quot;n&quot;] * sun).sum(-1), -1, 1)
    conc = sc.field(&quot;conc&quot;, lambda: T.concavity(max(T.cell * 3, 30)))
    cc = T.sample(conc, G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;])
    skyview = np.clip(0.55 + 0.45 * G[&quot;n&quot;][..., 2] + cc / 60.0, 0.05, 1.0)
    if bands is None:
        bands = [np.inf]
    band = band_of(G[&quot;t&quot;], bands)
    G.update(sunvis=sunvis, ndl=ndl, skyview=skyview, band=band)

    # ---- ground
    names, idx, Wt = ground_materials(sc, G, thr_b)
    for k, name in enumerate(names):
        m = G[&quot;hit&quot;] &amp; (idx == k)
        if not m.any():
            continue
        hard = name in (&quot;rock&quot;, &quot;snow&quot;)
        s = TG.slope_tone(ndl, sunvis, skyview, name, thr_4 if hard else thr_b, seam=0.22 if hard else 0.35)
        lit = (ndl &gt; 0.06) &amp; (sunvis &gt; 0.5)
        lo, hi = TG.family_bounds(name, lit)
        if name in (&quot;meadow&quot;, &quot;drygrass&quot;, &quot;soil&quot;, &quot;sand&quot;):
            s = TG.stipple(s, m, threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed + 3), up=0.16, down=0.16, lo=lo, hi=hi)
        if name in (&quot;rock&quot;, &quot;scree&quot;):
            s = TG.fall_line_flecks(s, m, G, threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed + 5), density=0.08, lit_ok=lit)
            s = np.clip(s, lo, hi)
        cv.fill(m, name, s, band=band)

    # ---- forest: texture where trees are sub-pixel, instances where they are big enough
    if &quot;forest&quot; in sc.cover and sc.trees is not None:
        fp = G[&quot;mpp&quot;]
        crown = np.median(sc.trees[&quot;w&quot;]) if len(sc.trees[&quot;w&quot;]) else 5.0
        fdens = T.sample(sc.cover[&quot;forest&quot;], G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;])
        tex = G[&quot;hit&quot;] &amp; (fdens &gt; 0.5 + (thr_b - 0.5) * 0.25) &amp; (crown / fp &lt; tex_threshold)
        if tex.any():
            TC.forest_texture(cv, tex, G, cam, sun, sunvis, ndl, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;,
                              tree_h=float(np.median(sc.trees[&quot;h&quot;])), crown_w=crown, seed=seed, band=band)
        big = sc.trees
        P = np.c_[big[&quot;x&quot;], big[&quot;y&quot;], big[&quot;z&quot;]]
        _, _, dep = cam.project(P)
        keep = big[&quot;w&quot;] / cam.footprint(dep) &gt;= tex_threshold
        sub = {k: v[keep] for k, v in big.items()}
        TC.paint_trees(cv, sub, cam, G, sun, lambda x, y: T.sample(shadow, x, y),
                       band_fn=(lambda t: int(band_of(t, bands))) if len(bands) &gt; 1 else None)

    pal = Palette(light)
    img, ncol = pal.render(cv.mat, cv.step, cv.band)
    info = dict(colours=ncol, G=G)
    if return_canvas:
        info[&quot;canvas&quot;] = cv
    return img, info

    onscr = (sx &gt; -40) &amp; (sx &lt; cv.w + 40) &amp; (sy &gt; -10) &amp; (sy &lt; cv.h + 120) &amp; (depth &gt; 0)

(the photo is box-downsampled to the same pixel scale first, so it is judged at the pixel size it
would have in the frame), with a caption strip.&quot;&quot;&quot;
import os
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import numpy as np

BG = (22, 22, 26)
FG = (230, 230, 220)


def _font(sz=14):
    for p in [&quot;/System/Library/Fonts/Menlo.ttc&quot;, &quot;/System/Library/Fonts/Monaco.ttf&quot;, &quot;/Library/Fonts/Arial.ttf&quot;]:
        if os.path.exists(p):
            try:
                return ImageFont.truetype(p, sz)
            except Exception:
                pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def px_photo(path, box, out_w):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;crop a photo and box-downsample so it is out_w pixels wide (what it looks like at that scale).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    im = Image.open(path).convert(&quot;RGB&quot;).crop(box)
    w, h = im.size
    return im.resize((out_w, max(1, round(h * out_w / w))), Image.BOX)


def up(im, k):
    return im.resize((im.size[0] * k, im.size[1] * k), Image.NEAREST)


def panel(title, ims, captions, k=8, note=None, gap=10):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;ims: list of small PIL images (native px); each shown at k x with a caption under it.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    f = _font(15); fs = _font(12)
    big = [up(i, k) if i.size[0] * k &lt;= 1400 else i for i in ims]
    H = max(b.size[1] for b in big)
    W = sum(b.size[0] for b in big) + gap * (len(big) + 1)
    top = 30; cap = 22; bot = 0 if not note else 18 * (note.count(&quot;\n&quot;) + 1) + 8
    out = Image.new(&quot;RGB&quot;, (W, top + H + cap + bot), BG)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(out)
    d.text((gap, 7), title, fill=FG, font=f)
    x = gap
    for b, c in zip(big, captions):
        out.paste(b, (x, top))
        d.text((x, top + H + 4), c, fill=(190, 190, 180), font=fs)
        x += b.size[0] + gap
    if note:
        d.multiline_text((gap, top + H + cap + 2), note, fill=(170, 170, 160), font=fs, spacing=4)
    return out


def stack(panels, gap=8):
    W = max(p.size[0] for p in panels); H = sum(p.size[1] for p in panels) + gap * (len(panels) - 1)
    out = Image.new(&quot;RGB&quot;, (W, H), BG); y = 0
    for p in panels:
        out.paste(p, (0, y)); y += p.size[1] + gap
    return out


def label(im, text, k=1):
    im = im.copy(); d = ImageDraw.Draw(im); f = _font(12)
    tw = d.textlength(text, font=f)
    d.rectangle([0, 0, tw + 6, 16], fill=(0, 0, 0)); d.text((3, 1), text, fill=(255, 255, 200), font=f)
    return im

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Spur-stroke conifer sprite, hue walk, rerun</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;
p=&#x27;tech_canopy.py&#x27;; s=open(p).read()
start=s.index(&#x27;def conifer_sprite&#x27;); end=s.index(&#x27;def broadleaf_sprite&#x27;)
new=&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;def conifer_sprite(w, h, sun, cam, seed=0, lit_ok=True, ramp_top=5):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a conifer crown w px wide, h px tall, built from Ferrari&#x27;s strokes: a 1-px spire, then tiers of
    horizontal spurs that droop at their outer ends and lengthen downward, lit on the sun side
    (ochre dab on the tip), dark gaps between tiers, the shade side in the reflected-light step.
    Conifer ramp: 0 gap | 1 shadow | 2 reflected || 3 halftone | 4 lit | 5 tip highlight.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;con2&quot;, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), round(sun[1], 2), round(sun[2], 2), seed % 6, lit_ok)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed % 6 + 100 * w + 7 * h)
    W = w + 2; Hh = h + 2
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32)
    cx = int(round((W - 1) / 2.0))
    sxs, _ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    side = 1 if sxs &gt;= 0 else -1
    hor = np.hypot(sun[0], sun[1]); elev = sun[2]
    pitch = 2 if h &lt; 14 else 3                  # rows per tier (spur row + gap rows)
    top = max(1, int(round(0.12 * h)))
    for yy in range(1, 1 + top):                # spire
        a[yy, cx] = True; st[yy, cx] = 4 if lit_ok else 2
    for yy in range(1 + top, 1 + h):
        v = (yy - 1) / max(h - 1, 1)
        r = 0.5 * (w - 1) * min(1.0, 0.18 + 0.95 * v)       # cone half-width
        spur = ((yy - 1 - top) % pitch) == 0
        rr = r if spur else r * 0.55                        # gap rows are narrower: the tier notch
        rr = rr * (0.9 + 0.2 * rng.random())
        xl = int(round(cx - rr)); xr = int(round(cx + rr))
        for xx in range(max(0, xl), min(W, xr + 1)):
            a[yy, xx] = True
            u = (xx - cx) / max(rr, 0.5)                    # -1 .. 1 across the crown
            sunside = u * side
            if spur:
                if lit_ok and (sunside &gt; -0.25 or (elev &gt; 0.55 and sunside &gt; -0.6)):
                    s = 3 + (sunside &gt; 0.2) + (sunside &gt; 0.75)
                    if v &gt; 0.8 and sunside &lt; 0.5:
                        s = min(s, 3)
                else:
                    s = 2 if sunside &gt; -0.8 else 1
            else:
                s = 0 if abs(u) &lt; 0.6 else 1
                if lit_ok and sunside &gt; 0.7 and v &lt; 0.5:
                    s = 2
            st[yy, xx] = s
        # droop: the spur&#x27;s outer ends hang one row lower
        if spur and yy + 1 &lt; Hh and r &gt;= 2:
            for xx in (xl, xr):
                if 0 &lt;= xx &lt; W:
                    a[yy + 1, xx] = True
                    st[yy + 1, xx] = st[yy, xx]
                    a[yy, xx] = rng.random() &lt; 0.5 and a[yy, xx]
    # trunk shadow: the axis below the spire is darker (the core of the tree)
    for yy in range(1 + top, 1 + h):
        if a[yy, cx] and st[yy, cx] &lt; 4:
            st[yy, cx] = max(0, st[yy, cx] - 1)
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, CON), step=st, ax=(W - 1) / 2.0, ay=Hh - 1.5)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr

new2=&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;def spire_mark(w, h, sun, cam, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;, lit_ok=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;2-4 px wide trees.  Conifer: a lit tip and a sun-side lit column over a dark body, with the
    tier notches every 2 rows (the smallest honest version of the spur stroke).  Broadleaf: a
    rounded dab, lit on the sun side of its top, dark below.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;spire2&quot;, kind, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), lit_ok)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    mat = CON if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else BRD
    W = max(1, w); Hh = max(2, h)
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32)
    sxs, _ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    side = 1 if sxs &gt;= 0 else -1
    c = (W - 1) / 2
    for yy in range(Hh):
        v = yy / max(Hh - 1, 1)
        if kind == &quot;conifer&quot;:
            half = c * min(1.0, 0.1 + v * 1.4)
            notch = (yy % 2 == 1) and yy &gt; 1 and W &gt;= 3
        else:
            half = c * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(np.pi * min(1, v * 1.15))); notch = False
        for xx in range(W):
            d = xx - c
            if abs(d) &lt;= half + 0.01 and not (notch and abs(d) &gt; half - 0.6):
                a[yy, xx] = True
                sunside = d * side &gt;= -0.01 if W &gt; 1 else True
                if lit_ok and yy == 0:
                    st[yy, xx] = 4
                elif lit_ok and sunside and v &lt; 0.75:
                    st[yy, xx] = 4 if (v &lt; 0.35 and d * side &gt; 0) else 3
                elif lit_ok and v &lt; 0.3:
                    st[yy, xx] = 3
                else:
                    st[yy, xx] = 1 if v &lt; 0.9 else 0
                if not lit_ok:
                    st[yy, xx] = 2 if (yy == 0 or (sunside and v &lt; 0.4)) else (1 if v &lt; 0.9 else 0)
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, mat), step=st, ax=c, ay=Hh - 1)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr

def walk_hue(rgb, deg, ns):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Ferrari&#x27;s hue walk along a ramp: shadow steps rotate cooler, lit steps warmer (toward ochre).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    L = lab(rgb)
    n = len(rgb)
    for k in range(n):
        t = (k - (ns - 0.5)) / max(n / 2, 1)          # &lt;0 shadow, &gt;0 light
        a, b = L[k, 1], L[k, 2]
        ang = np.radians(-deg * t)                      # positive t: rotate toward yellow/orange (clockwise from green)
        L[k, 1] = a * np.cos(ang) - b * np.sin(ang)
        L[k, 2] = a * np.sin(ang) + b * np.cos(ang)
    return from_lab(L)&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;)

new=&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;def conifer_sprite(w, h, sun, cam, seed=0, lit_ok=True, occl=0.8, tone=0):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a conifer crown w px wide, h px tall.  Two layers:
      * the lit-cap gradient (the photos): the top of the crown is lit, value falls toward the base
        because neighbours shade it (occl = stand density 0..1: a lone tree stays lit to the ground);
        the sun side is one step above the shade side;
      * Ferrari&#x27;s strokes on top: 1-px spire, tiers of horizontal spurs that droop at their outer
        ends, a tip highlight on the sun-side spur ends, the tier notches one step darker.
    Conifer ramp: 0 gap | 1 shadow | 2 reflected || 3 halftone | 4 lit | 5 tip highlight.
    tone: -1/0/+1 per-tree value variation (species, age), applied inside the families.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    key = (&quot;con3&quot;, w, h, round(sun[0], 2), round(sun[1], 2), round(sun[2], 2), seed % 6, lit_ok, round(occl, 1), tone)
    if key in _cache:
        return _cache[key]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed % 6 + 100 * w + 7 * h)
    W = w + 2; Hh = h + 2
    a = np.zeros((Hh, W), bool); st = np.zeros((Hh, W), np.int32)
    cx = int(round((W - 1) / 2.0))
    sxs, _ = _screen_sun(sun, cam)
    side = 1 if sxs &gt;= 0 else -1
    elev = sun[2]
    pitch = 2 if h &lt; 16 else 3
    top = max(1, int(round(0.1 * h)))
    phase = rng.integers(0, pitch)
    for yy in range(1, 1 + top):                # spire
        a[yy, cx] = True; st[yy, cx] = 4 if lit_ok else 2
    for yy in range(1 + top, 1 + h):
        v = (yy - 1) / max(h - 1, 1)
        r = 0.5 * (w - 1) * min(1.0, 0.2 + 1.0 * v)
        spur = ((yy - 1 - top + phase) % pitch) == 0
        rr = r if spur else r * 0.7
        rr = rr * (0.88 + 0.24 * rng.random())
        xl = int(round(cx - rr)); xr = int(round(cx + rr))
        # the lit-cap gradient: light available at this height
        avail = 1.0 - occl * np.clip((v - 0.25) / 0.6, 0, 1)
        for xx in range(max(0, xl), min(W, xr + 1)):
            a[yy, xx] = True
            u = (xx - cx) / max(rr, 0.5)
            sunside = u * side
            if lit_ok:
                lvl = avail * (0.62 + 0.38 * sunside + 0.25 * elev) + 0.12 * tone
                if lvl &gt; 0.95:   s = 5
                elif lvl &gt; 0.72: s = 4
                elif lvl &gt; 0.48: s = 3
                elif lvl &gt; 0.3:  s = 2
                elif lvl &gt; 0.15: s = 1
                else:            s = 0
            else:
                lvl = avail * (0.6 + 0.3 * sunside) + 0.1 * tone
                s = 2 if lvl &gt; 0.62 else (1 if lvl &gt; 0.3 else 0)
            if not spur and s &gt; 0 and abs(u) &gt; 0.2:
                s = s - 1 if s != 3 else 2      # tier notch: one step down (and across the terminator only 3-&gt;2)
            st[yy, xx] = s
        # spur tips: highlight on the sun-side end, droop one row
        if spur and r &gt;= 1.5:
            tip = xr if side &gt; 0 else xl
            if lit_ok and 0 &lt;= tip &lt; W and avail &gt; 0.45:
                st[yy, tip] = 5 if avail &gt; 0.7 else 4
            if yy + 1 &lt; 1 + h:
                for xx in (xl, xr):
                    if 0 &lt;= xx &lt; W:
                        a[yy + 1, xx] = True; st[yy + 1, xx] = st[yy, xx]
    spr = dict(a=a, mat=np.full(a.shape, CON), step=st, ax=(W - 1) / 2.0, ay=Hh - 1.5)
    _cache[key] = spr
    return spr

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Add canopy-surface functions</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; cat &gt;&gt; tech_canopy.py &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;


# --------------------------------------------------------------------- canopy surface (far regime)
def canopy_height(T, trees, smooth_below=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;rasterise every crown as a bump (conifer: cone, broadleaf: dome) into a height grid on the
    terrain&#x27;s cells, max-composited.  Where crowns are smaller than ~2 cells they are rasterised
    as single-cell cones anyway: the forest surface then carries crown-scale grain.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    C = np.zeros_like(T.H)
    fi, fj = T.ij(trees[&quot;x&quot;], trees[&quot;y&quot;])
    cell = T.cell
    for kind in (0, 1):
        sel = np.nonzero(trees[&quot;kind&quot;] == kind)[0]
        if len(sel) == 0:
            continue
        rmax = int(np.ceil(trees[&quot;w&quot;][sel].max() / 2 / cell)) + 1
        for dj in range(-rmax, rmax + 1):
            for di in range(-rmax, rmax + 1):
                jj = np.round(fj[sel]).astype(int) + dj; ii = np.round(fi[sel]).astype(int) + di
                ok = (jj &gt;= 0) &amp; (jj &lt; T.ny) &amp; (ii &gt;= 0) &amp; (ii &lt; T.nx)
                d = np.hypot((ii - fi[sel]) * cell, (jj - fj[sel]) * cell) / (trees[&quot;w&quot;][sel] / 2)
                if kind == 0:
                    hgt = trees[&quot;h&quot;][sel] * (1 - 0.55 * np.clip(d, 0, 1.2))      # cone top: pointed
                else:
                    hgt = trees[&quot;h&quot;][sel] * (0.75 + 0.25 * np.sqrt(np.clip(1 - d * d, 0, 1)))  # dome
                hgt = np.where(d &lt;= 1.0, hgt, 0.0)
                ok &amp;= hgt &gt; 0
                np.maximum.at(C, (jj[ok], ii[ok]), hgt[ok])
    return C


def paint_canopy_surface(cv, mask, G, sun, sunvis, ndl, skyview, apex, pit, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;, band=None,
                         thr=None, seed=0):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;the far forest, from the canopy surface&#x27;s own light: lit family where the crown faces the sun,
    shadow family in gaps and on the shade side, then the marks: a highlight dab on sunlit crown apices
    with the stem pixel below it one step down (contrast inside the stroke), and the darkest step in
    the pits between crowns (dark holes).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    mat = CON if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else BRD
    h, w = mask.shape
    if thr is None:
        thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    lit = (ndl &gt; 0.05) &amp; (sunvis &gt; 0.5)
    # lit family 3..5 by n.l; shadow family 0..2 by skyview
    xl = 3 + np.clip((ndl - 0.05) / 0.75, 0, 1) * 1.6
    xs = np.clip((skyview - 0.35) / 0.55, 0, 1) * 2.4
    x = np.where(lit, xl, xs)
    s = np.floor(x + (thr - 0.5) * 0.6).astype(np.int32)
    s = np.where(lit, np.clip(s, 3, 5), np.clip(s, 0, 2))
    # marks
    ap = mask &amp; apex
    s = np.where(ap &amp; lit, np.minimum(s + 1, 5), s)
    s = np.where(ap &amp; ~lit, np.minimum(s + 1, 2), s)
    below = np.zeros_like(ap); below[1:] = ap[:-1]
    s = np.where(below &amp; mask &amp; ~ap, np.maximum(s - 1, np.where(lit, 3, 0)), s)
    s = np.where(pit &amp; mask, 0, s)
    cv.fill(mask, mat, s, band=band, tag=1)
EOF

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/subjects.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;subjects.py -- small world builders for the studies and plates (what the simulator would own).

Each returns a painter.Scene.  Sizes in metres; `cell` chooses the grid resolution (the canopy
surface needs cells &lt;= crown/3 when crowns are drawn as a surface).
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
from terrain import Terrain
import landform as LF
import tech_canopy as TC
from painter import Scene


def grid(size_x, size_y, cell):
    nx = int(size_x / cell) + 1; ny = int(size_y / cell) + 1
    Y, X = np.mgrid[0:ny, 0:nx] * cell
    return X.astype(float), Y.astype(float)


def forest_slope(size=600, cell=1.5, seed=1, slope=0.25, clearing=(0.5, 0.5, 60), broadleaf=0.0,
                 spacing=5.0, hmean=20.0, lone_trees=12):
    X, Y = grid(size, size, cell)
    H = slope * Y + 0.07 * size * LF.eroded_fbm(X, Y, size * 0.7, octaves=6, seed=seed + 1) + 3 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 40, seed=seed + 4)
    T = Terrain(H, cell)
    cx, cy, cr = clearing[0] * size, clearing[1] * size, clearing[2]
    rr = np.hypot(X - cx, (Y - cy) * 1.2)
    edge = cr * (1 + 0.35 * LF.fbm(X, Y, cr * 0.8, seed=seed + 9))
    clear = rr &lt; edge
    stand = np.clip(0.85 + 0.8 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 45, seed=seed + 3), 0, 1)
    forest = np.where(clear, 0.0, 1.0)
    cover = dict(meadow=np.where(clear, 1.0, 0.4), forest=forest)
    if broadleaf:
        cover[&quot;broadleaf&quot;] = np.clip(broadleaf + 0.8 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 120, seed=seed + 5), 0, 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    trees = TC.scatter_trees(T, forest * stand, spacing, rng, kind_field=cover.get(&quot;broadleaf&quot;), hmean=hmean)
    # a few lone trees standing in the clearing (for cast shadows)
    if lone_trees:
        ang = rng.random(lone_trees) * 2 * np.pi; rad = np.sqrt(rng.random(lone_trees)) * cr * 0.7
        lx = cx + np.cos(ang) * rad; ly = cy + np.sin(ang) * rad
        lt = dict(x=lx, y=ly, z=T.height(lx, ly), h=hmean * (0.6 + 0.4 * rng.random(lone_trees)),
                  kind=(rng.random(lone_trees) &lt; broadleaf).astype(int), seed=rng.integers(0, 1 &lt;&lt; 20, lone_trees))
        lt[&quot;w&quot;] = np.where(lt[&quot;kind&quot;] == 0, lt[&quot;h&quot;] * 0.33, lt[&quot;h&quot;] * 0.6)
        trees = {k: np.concatenate([trees[k], lt[k]]) for k in trees}
    return Scene(T, cover, trees=trees, seed=seed)

    def to_shadow(self, mask):
        &quot;&quot;&quot;move painted pixels under `mask` from the lit family into the shadow family, keeping their
        texture: the dominant lit steps become the top shadow step, the lower ones the step below.&quot;&quot;&quot;
        for m in np.unique(self.mat[mask]):
            if m == 0:
                continue
            n = MATERIALS[MAT_NAME[m]][&quot;steps&quot;]; ns = n // 2
            sel = mask &amp; (self.mat == m) &amp; (self.step &gt;= ns)
            self.step[sel] = np.where(self.step[sel] &gt;= ns + 1, ns - 1, max(ns - 2, 0))

downsampled to the same crown size in pixels) and the nearest Ferrari.

    nice -n 10 python3 study_canopy.py         -&gt; studies/canopy.png
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import os, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), &quot;lib&quot;))
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from camera import Iso
from pal import LIGHTS
from painter import paint
from subjects import forest_slope
import study as S

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
R = lambda p: os.path.join(ROOT, &quot;refs&quot;, p)
DEER = R(&quot;aerial_view_forested_mountain_slope/commons_Deer_Mountain_Village_at_Dawn_jpg_a363c568.jpg&quot;)
ALSEK = R(&quot;aerial_conifer_forest_hillside/openverse_Between_Dangerous_River_and_Alsek_River_c0688f25.jpg&quot;)
NRCS = R(&quot;aerial_view_forested_mountain_slope/openverse_20120720_NRCS_LSC_0489_1cd3d6e1.jpg&quot;)
FER = lambda n: os.path.join(ROOT, &quot;refs/ferrari_lw&quot;, n + &quot;.png&quot;)

W, H = 96, 64      # native pixels per study cell


def mine(mpp, seed=1, light=&quot;afternoon&quot;, broadleaf=0.0, cell=None, target=(0.5, 0.47)):
    crown = 20 * 0.33
    cell = cell or min(1.5, max(0.5, crown / 3.5))
    size = max(260, mpp * W * 2.6)
    sc = forest_slope(size=size, cell=cell, seed=seed, clearing=(0.5, 0.5, max(18, mpp * 16)), broadleaf=broadleaf)
    tx, ty = target[0] * size, target[1] * size
    cam = Iso(target=(tx, ty, sc.T.height(tx, ty)), mpp=mpp, elev=30, yaw=0, w=W, h=H)
    img, info = paint(sc, cam, LIGHTS[light])
    return Image.fromarray(img), info[&quot;colours&quot;]


def run():
    rows = []
    # row 1: crowns ~10 px (0.66 m/px): sprite regime
    a, n1 = mine(0.66)
    p = S.px_photo(DEER, (0, 600, 380, 853), W)                 # crown ~38 px at full res -&gt; ~10 px
    f = Image.open(FER(&quot;V16&quot;)).convert(&quot;RGB&quot;).crop((520, 170, 520 + W, 170 + H))
    rows.append(S.panel(&quot;canopy, crowns ~10 px (0.66 m/px): tiered spur strokes on a lit-cap gradient&quot;,
                        [p, a, f], [&quot;photo (Deer Mtn, dawn) at the same crown px&quot;, f&quot;mine ({n1} colours)&quot;, &quot;Ferrari V16 conifers&quot;]))
    # row 2: crowns ~4 px (1.6 m/px): sprite regime, spire marks
    b, n2 = mine(1.6)
    p = S.px_photo(ALSEK, (0, 380, 580, 767), W)
    f = Image.open(FER(&quot;V16&quot;)).convert(&quot;RGB&quot;).crop((300, 150, 300 + W, 150 + H))
    rows.append(S.panel(&quot;canopy, crowns ~4 px (1.6 m/px): lit-cap arrowheads over dark notches&quot;,
                        [p, b, f], [&quot;photo (Alsek, overcast) same crown px&quot;, f&quot;mine ({n2})&quot;, &quot;Ferrari V16 far treeline&quot;]))
    # row 3: crowns ~1.5 px (4.4 m/px): canopy surface regime
    c, n3 = mine(4.4)
    p = S.px_photo(NRCS, (380, 0, 1024, 430), W)
    f = Image.open(FER(&quot;V14&quot;)).convert(&quot;RGB&quot;).crop((0, 370, W, 370 + H))
    rows.append(S.panel(&quot;canopy, crowns ~1.5 px (4.4 m/px): canopy as a lit surface + apex dabs + dark pits&quot;,
                        [p, c, f], [&quot;photo (NRCS far slope) same scale&quot;, f&quot;mine ({n3})&quot;, &quot;Ferrari V14 lower forest&quot;]))
    out = S.stack(rows)
    out.save(os.path.join(ROOT, &quot;studies&quot;, &quot;canopy.png&quot;))
    print(&quot;studies/canopy.png&quot;, out.size)


if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    run()

# surface painting: gentler shade side, rarer pits, far-regime marks

s=s[:start]+&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;def paint_canopy_surface(cv, mask, G, sun, sunvis, ndl, skyview, apex, pit, kind=&quot;conifer&quot;, band=None,
                         thr=None, seed=0, crown_px=3.0):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;the far forest, from the canopy surface&#x27;s own light.
      * lit family (3..5) where the crown surface faces the sun; the shade side of a crown is the
        reflected-light step (2), not black: in every canopy photo the dark fraction is only ~20-25%
        (narrow seams, spacing / lit radius 2.3-2.5), the rest is lit cap or lit-from-sky flank;
      * the darkest steps only in the pits between crowns and where terrain shadow covers the mass;
      * marks: sunlit crown apices get the highlight with the pixel below one step down (the stroke&#x27;s
        own contrast).  When crowns are under ~2.5 px the surface is too coarse to carry apices, so
        the marks become a blue-noise stipple of 1x2 dashes (lit head, darker stem) at density
        0.22 -- grain that stays one pixel, never a pattern.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    mat = CON if kind == &quot;conifer&quot; else BRD
    h, w = mask.shape
    if thr is None:
        thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    lit = (ndl &gt; 0.0) &amp; (sunvis &gt; 0.5)
    xl = 3 + np.clip(ndl / 0.8, 0, 1) * 1.4
    xs = 1.0 + np.clip((skyview - 0.3) / 0.6, 0, 1) * 1.4
    x = np.where(lit, xl, xs)
    s = np.floor(x + (thr - 0.5) * 0.7).astype(np.int32)
    s = np.where(lit, np.clip(s, 3, 4), np.clip(s, 0, 2))
    if crown_px &gt;= 2.5:
        ap = mask &amp; apex
        s = np.where(ap &amp; lit, 5, s)
        s = np.where(ap &amp; ~lit, np.maximum(s, 2), s)
        below = np.zeros_like(ap); below[1:] = ap[:-1]
        s = np.where(below &amp; mask &amp; ~ap, np.where(lit, 3, np.maximum(s - 1, 0)), s)
        s = np.where(pit &amp; mask, np.where(sunvis &gt; 0.5, 1, 0), s)
    else:
        t2 = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed + 23)
        head = mask &amp; (t2 &lt; 0.22)
        s = np.where(head &amp; lit, np.minimum(s + 1, 5), np.where(head, np.maximum(s, 2), s))
        stem = np.zeros_like(head); stem[1:] = head[:-1]
        s = np.where(stem &amp; mask &amp; ~head, np.where(lit, 3, np.maximum(s - 1, 0)), s)
        dark = mask &amp; (t2 &gt; 0.9)
        s = np.where(dark, np.where(lit, 2, 0), s)
    cv.fill(mask, mat, s, band=band, tag=1)

def water_ramp(light, body=(28, 52, 58)):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;water is the sky seen in a dark mirror.  0 shaded bank lip | 1 reflection of land/trees |
    2 water body | 3 low-sky sheen | 4 bright sky (grazing) | 5 glint.  Colours come from the sky
    and the water body, not from an albedo under light.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    b = np.array(body, float) * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.mean(light.sky_hor) / 200)
    t, h = light.sky_top, light.sky_hor
    sun = np.clip(lin_to_srgb(srgb_to_lin(h) * 1.6), 0, 255)
    cols = np.array([b * 0.45, b * 0.8 + t * 0.05, b * 0.85 + t * 0.25, b * 0.45 + t * 0.6, b * 0.15 + h * 0.9, sun * 0.3 + 255 * 0.7])
    return push_chroma(np.clip(cols, 0, 255), 1.2)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- materials&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;)
s=s.replace(&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;    E = step_irradiance(n, light)
    col = alb[None] * E * light.exposure&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;,&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;    if mat == &quot;water&quot;:
        return water_ramp(light)
    E = step_irradiance(n, light)

What the photos say (measure/photo_stats.json &#x27;river_*&#x27;, Cosumnes drone, Minnesota wetland,
Tierra del Fuego valley):
  * water from above is a dark mirror.  Looking steeply down (near, ~40 deg) the river is DARKER
    than the land around it (L* 23 vs canopy 53, sunny field 68); toward grazing angles (mid/far)
    it turns to sky (L* 45, chroma 32, sky hue).  Fresnel does this: 5% at 30 deg down, 18% at 15.
  * the far bank&#x27;s trees reflect as a dark band hanging down into the water from the far bank
    (in ortho iso the band is exactly the trees&#x27; projected height, mirrored).
  * the near bank has a dark lip; the far waterline at grazing angles a light line.
  * flow reads as sparse streaks along the current; still water as flat bands.
What Ferrari does (V02 rivers from high up, V11AM, V16 pond): water is the lightest thing on the
land at a distance -- sky colours in flat bands joined by checker seams; meanders foreshorten into
long horizontal runs with short vertical steps; near water sparkles with a 2-colour stipple of the
hottest sky colours; the upper bank edge carries a dark 1-px line.
Stylization applied: Fresnel reflectance x2.5 (Ferrari&#x27;s water is always more sky than physics
allows; his reflections drop only ~13 L* from what they mirror).
Water ramp (pal.water_ramp): 0 lip | 1 land reflection | 2 body | 3 low-sky sheen | 4 bright sky | 5 glint.
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from dither import threshold_map, flat_bands

WATER_BOOST = 2.5


def fresnel(cos_i, f0=0.02):
    return f0 + (1 - f0) * (1 - np.clip(cos_i, 0, 1)) ** 5


def paint_water(cv, mask, G, Trefl, sun, light, seed=0, band=None, flow=0.0, sky_val=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;mask: water pixels.  Trefl: terrain (+canopy) used to trace the reflected rays.
    flow in [0,1]: 0 still (lake), 1 river (streaks along the current).&quot;&quot;&quot;
    from pal import MAT_ID
    h, w = mask.shape
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    step = np.zeros((h, w), np.int32)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return step
    D = G[&quot;D&quot;][ys, xs]
    R = D.copy(); R[:, 2] = -R[:, 2]
    P = np.c_[G[&quot;x&quot;][ys, xs], G[&quot;y&quot;][ys, xs], G[&quot;z&quot;][ys, xs] + 0.3]
    t = Trefl.raymarch(P[None], R[None], t0=0.5, t1=3000)[0]
    land = np.isfinite(t)
    # what the reflected ray hits: land (dark, a little lighter if that land is sunlit) or sky
    el = np.arcsin(np.clip(R[:, 2], 0, 1))                  # sky elevation seen in the mirror
    F = np.clip(fresnel(R[:, 2]) * WATER_BOOST, 0, 1)
    thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;bayer4&quot;)[ys, xs]
    # continuous coordinate: 2 (body) .. 4 (bright sky) by F; sky near the horizon is brighter
    skyb = 0.55 + 0.45 * (1 - el / (np.pi / 2))
    x = 2.0 + np.clip(F * skyb * 5.0, 0, 2.2)
    s = flat_bands(x, thr, 0.25)
    s = np.where(land, 1, s)
    s = np.clip(s, 1, 4)
    step[ys, xs] = s
    # ---- marks
    thr_b = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    below = np.zeros_like(mask); below[:-1] = mask[1:]        # pixel below is water
    above = np.zeros_like(mask); above[1:] = mask[:-1]
    near_lip = mask &amp; ~below                                  # lowest water row: near bank lip
    far_edge = mask &amp; ~above                                  # top water row: far waterline
    step[near_lip] = 0
    fe = far_edge &amp; (step &gt;= 2)
    step[fe] = np.minimum(step[fe] + 1, 4)                    # light line at the far waterline
    # streaks along the current (river) / wind lines (lake): horizontal 2-5 px dashes one step lighter
    if flow &gt; 0 or True:
        dens = 0.015 + 0.05 * flow
        seeds = mask &amp; (thr_b &lt; dens) &amp; (step &gt;= 1) &amp; (step &lt;= 3)
        yy, xx = np.nonzero(seeds)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        L = rng.integers(2, 6, len(yy))
        for y, x, l in zip(yy, xx, L):
            for k in range(l):
                if x + k &lt; w and mask[y, x + k] and step[y, x + k] in (1, 2, 3):
                    step[y, x + k] += 1
    # glint when the sun is mirrored toward the camera (back light)
    Rs = np.array([sun[0], sun[1], -sun[2]])
    gl = (D @ Rs) if D.ndim == 2 else None
    if gl is not None:
        g = np.zeros((h, w)); g[ys, xs] = gl
        glint = mask &amp; (g &gt; 0.985) &amp; (thr_b &lt; 0.5)
        step[glint] = 5
    cv.fill(mask, &quot;water&quot;, step, band=band, tag=2)
    return step
</pre></details><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Integrate water into painter</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;

    if sc.trees is not None and forest_mode == &quot;surface&quot;:&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;,&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;    C = None
    from terrain import Terrain
    Wl = None
    if sc.water is not None:
        Wl = np.where(np.isfinite(sc.water[&quot;level&quot;]), sc.water[&quot;level&quot;], -1e9)
    if sc.trees is not None and forest_mode == &quot;surface&quot;:&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;)

    else:

s=s.replace(&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;    # ---- forest floor under the canopy&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;,&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;    # ---- water: the dark mirror
    if Wl is not None:
        import tech_water as TW
        wl = T0.sample(Wl, G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;])
        wmask = G[&quot;hit&quot;] &amp; (wl &gt; T0.sample(T0.H, G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;]) + 0.02) &amp; (np.abs(G[&quot;z&quot;] - wl) &lt; 0.5)
        if sc.trees is not None:
            Cr = C if C is not None else sc.field((&quot;canopy_refl&quot;,), lambda: TC.canopy_height(T0, sc.trees))
            Trefl = sc.field((&quot;Trefl&quot;,), lambda: Terrain(T0.H + Cr, T0.cell, T0.x0, T0.y0))
        else:
            Trefl = T0
        TW.paint_water(cv, wmask, G, Trefl, sun, light, seed=seed, band=band, flow=sc.water.get(&quot;flow&quot;, 0.0))
        G[&quot;water&quot;] = wmask
    # ---- forest floor under the canopy&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;)
open(p,&#x27;w&#x27;).write(s)

def river_valley(size=900, cell=1.5, seed=2, width=14.0, meander=0.18, forest_sides=True, spacing=5.5,
                 hmean=20.0, valley_w=0.35, broadleaf=0.3):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a flat-floored valley running north with a meandering river on meadows, forest on the sides.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    X, Y = grid(size, size, cell)
    cxv = size * 0.5 + size * 0.08 * LF.fbm(X * 0 + 1, Y, size * 0.8, seed=seed)       # valley axis
    d = (X - cxv) / (size * valley_w)
    H = 0.05 * Y + 90 * np.clip(np.abs(d) - 0.5, 0, None) ** 1.5 + 25 * LF.eroded_fbm(X, Y, size * 0.5, seed=seed + 1) * np.clip(np.abs(d) - 0.3, 0, 1) \
        + 0.6 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 25, seed=seed + 2)
    # meander: river centreline x(y)
    yy = Y[:, 0]
    rx = cxv[:, 0] + size * meander * 0.5 * np.sin(yy / (size * 0.22) * 2 * np.pi + 1.0) + size * 0.05 * LF.fbm(np.zeros_like(yy), yy, size * 0.3, seed=seed + 7)
    dist = np.abs(X - rx[:, None])
    # channel distance should be measured perpendicular to the path: correct by the path slope
    slope = np.gradient(rx, cell)
    dist = dist / np.sqrt(1 + slope[:, None] ** 2)
    wloc = width * (1 + 0.25 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 80, seed=seed + 8))
    bed = np.clip(1 - dist / (wloc / 2 + 2.0), 0, 1)
    H = H - 1.8 * np.sqrt(bed)
    level = np.where(dist &lt; wloc / 2, H.max() * 0 + (0.05 * Y + 0.0 - 0.4 + 0.6 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 25, seed=seed + 2) * 0), np.nan)
    # water level: floor height at the centreline minus a little, smooth along y
    floor = 0.05 * Y - 0.5
    level = np.where(dist &lt; wloc / 2, floor, np.nan)
    T = Terrain(H, cell)
    side = np.clip((np.abs(d) - 0.42) / 0.12, 0, 1)
    patch = LF.fbm(X, Y, 90, seed=seed + 5)
    forest = np.clip(side + (patch &gt; 0.35) * 0.9, 0, 1) * (dist &gt; wloc / 2 + 6)
    forest = (forest &gt; 0.5).astype(float)
    cover = dict(meadow=np.ones_like(H) * 0.6, soil=np.where(dist &lt; wloc / 2 + 2.5, 0.7, 0.0), forest=forest)
    cover[&quot;broadleaf&quot;] = np.clip(broadleaf + 0.6 * patch + 0.5 * (np.abs(d) &lt; 0.45), 0, 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    trees = TC.scatter_trees(T, forest, spacing, rng, kind_field=cover[&quot;broadleaf&quot;], hmean=hmean)
    return Scene(T, cover, trees=trees, water=dict(level=level, flow=1.0), seed=seed)
EOF

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Tune water, point bars, broadleaf colour</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;

s=s.replace(&quot;WATER_BOOST = 2.5&quot;,&quot;WATER_BOOST = 1.8&quot;)

import os, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), &quot;lib&quot;))
from PIL import Image
from camera import Iso
from pal import LIGHTS

from subjects import river_valley
import study as S

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
R = lambda p: os.path.join(ROOT, &quot;refs&quot;, p)
COS = R(&quot;drone_river_meadow_valley/commons_Cosumnes_River_drone_jpg_d9b23c68.jpg&quot;)
MINN = R(&quot;aerial_river_valley_meadow/commons_Minnesota_River_Valley_Wetland_jpg_a3bd8e9a.jpg&quot;)

def run(light=&quot;afternoon&quot;):
    sc = river_valley(seed=2)
    rows = []
    for mpp, photo, box, cap, fer, fbox, fcap, title in [
        (0.8, COS, (160, 300, 880, 780), &quot;photo (Cosumnes, drone) same river px&quot;, &quot;V02&quot;, (60, 280, 156, 344), &quot;Ferrari V02 near river&quot;,
         &quot;river ribbon, 0.8 m/px: dark mirror body, sky sheen streaks, near-bank lip, far waterline light&quot;),
        (2.0, MINN, (250, 560, 1010, 1067), &quot;photo (Minnesota wetland) meander&quot;, &quot;V11AM&quot;, (130, 330, 226, 394), &quot;Ferrari V11AM river&quot;,

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Cirque subject builder and test</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; cat &gt;&gt; subjects.py &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;


def cirque(size=1000, cell=2.0, seed=3, snow=0.5, erode=True, trees=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a glacial cirque opening south toward the camera: headwall with cliff bands and gullies, scree
    cones under the gullies, a tarn on the floor, snow in shaded hollows.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    X, Y = grid(size, size, cell)
    cx, cy, R = size * 0.5, size * 0.58, size * 0.3
    dx, dy = X - cx, Y - cy
    r = np.hypot(dx, dy * 1.1); th = np.arctan2(dx, dy)          # th = 0 north (back wall)
    open_ = np.clip(np.cos(th) * 0.8 + 0.45, 0, 1)                 # walls high at the back, open to the south
    wall = smooth = np.clip((r - R * 0.55) / (R * 0.6), 0, 1)
    H = 320 * (wall ** 1.6) * open_ + 0.04 * (cy - Y) * -1
    H += 18 * LF.eroded_fbm(X, Y, 300, octaves=6, seed=seed)
    # gullies: radial grooves in the headwall
    gul = LF.ridged(th[..., None].squeeze() * 90 + 0 * X, r * 0.02, 1.0, octaves=2, seed=seed + 1)
    H -= 22 * (1 - gul) * np.clip((r - R * 0.6) / R, 0, 1) * open_
    # cliff bands: ledges where it is steep (terrace the height on the walls)
    steep0 = np.clip((wall - 0.15) * 3, 0, 1) * open_
    band = 22.0
    Ht = np.floor(H / band) * band + band * np.clip((H / band % 1 - 0.35) / 0.65, 0, 1) ** 2
    H = H * (1 - 0.6 * steep0) + Ht * 0.6 * steep0
    # floor: a shallow basin for the tarn
    H -= 12 * np.exp(-((dx / (R * 0.45)) ** 2 + ((dy + R * 0.15) / (R * 0.32)) ** 2))
    H += 3 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 40, seed=seed + 5)
    if erode:
        H = LF.droplet_erosion(H, cell, n_drops=150000, seed=seed)
    T = Terrain(H, cell)
    slope = T.slope_deg()
    conc = T.concavity(30)
    # tarn: fill the basin up to a level
    lvl = np.percentile(H[r &lt; R * 0.5], 12)
    lab, n = ndi.label(H &lt; lvl)
    ci, cj = int(cx / cell), int((cy - R * 0.15) / cell)
    tarn = lab == lab[cj, ci] if lab[cj, ci] else np.zeros_like(H, bool)
    level = np.where(tarn, lvl, np.nan)
    cliff = np.clip((slope - 38) / 8, 0, 1)
    # scree: moderately steep ground below the cliffs (and on the eroded fans)
    below_cliff = ndi.maximum_filter(cliff &gt; 0.5, size=int(90 / cell)) &amp; (slope &gt; 18) &amp; (slope &lt; 42)
    scree = below_cliff.astype(float) * 0.9
    rough = LF.fbm(X, Y, 50, seed=seed + 6)
    meadow = np.clip(1 - slope / 30, 0, 1) * 0.8
    shade = 1 - T.sun_shadow(np.array([-0.5, -0.3, 0.8]) / np.linalg.norm([-0.5, -0.3, 0.8]))   # a neutral &#x27;north-ish shade&#x27; proxy
    snow_w = np.clip(snow * 1.4 - 0.3 + (-conc / 6.0) + 0.5 * (H - H.mean()) / np.ptp(H) + 0.3 * rough, 0, 1) * (slope &lt; 44)
    cover = dict(meadow=meadow, scree=scree, rock=cliff * 1.2 + 0.25, snow=(snow_w &gt; 0.55).astype(float) * 1.3)
    forest = ((slope &lt; 25) &amp; (r &gt; R * 0.7) &amp; (H &lt; np.percentile(H, 35)) &amp; (rough &gt; 0.05)).astype(float)
    cover[&quot;forest&quot;] = forest
    tr = None
    if trees:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        tr = TC.scatter_trees(T, forest * 0.8, 6.0, rng, hmean=16)
    extras = dict(cliff=cliff, conc=conc)
    return Scene(T, cover, trees=tr, water=dict(level=level, flow=0.0), extras=extras, seed=seed)
EOF

from subjects import cirque

T=sc.T; s=T.slope_deg()

names=[&#x27;meadow&#x27;,&#x27;scree&#x27;,&#x27;rock&#x27;,&#x27;snow&#x27;,&#x27;forest&#x27;]

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Rebuild cirque by radial profile</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;
p=&#x27;subjects.py&#x27;; s=open(p).read()
start=s.index(&#x27;def cirque(&#x27;); s=s[:start]
s+=&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;def cirque(size=1000, cell=2.0, seed=3, snow=0.5, erode=True, trees=True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;a glacial cirque opening south toward the camera, built from its radial profile:
    tarn floor | scree apron (30 deg) with cones under the gullies | headwall (55-65 deg) with cliff
    bands | crest.  Snow lies in the gullies, on ledges and at the top of the apron in shade.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    X, Y = grid(size, size, cell)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cx, cy = size * 0.5, size * 0.56
    dx, dy = X - cx, Y - cy
    r = np.hypot(dx, dy); th = np.arctan2(dx, dy)                     # th = 0: back wall (north)
    wob = LF.fbm(np.cos(th) * 3, np.sin(th) * 3, 1.0, seed=seed)      # slow wobble of the wall line
    # gullies: notches at random angles
    ng = 13; ga = np.sort(rng.uniform(-2.3, 2.3, ng)); gw = rng.uniform(0.035, 0.07, ng); gd = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.0, ng)
    g = np.zeros_like(r)
    for a, w_, d in zip(ga, gw, gd):
        g = np.maximum(g, d * np.exp(-0.5 * ((th - a) / w_) ** 2))
    R0 = 150 + 25 * wob - 45 * g                   # apron toe (cones reach further out under gullies)
    R1 = 245 + 30 * wob + 25 * g                   # headwall foot
    R2 = 350 + 25 * wob + 35 * g                   # crest
    open_ = np.clip(np.cos(th) * 0.75 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0) ** 0.8
    apron = np.clip((r - R0) / (R1 - R0), 0, 1)
    h_apron = 0.6 * (R1 - R0) * apron ** 1.3 * (1 + 0.4 * g)        # concave-up cones
    wallt = np.clip((r - R1) / (R2 - R1), 0, 1)
    h_wall = 1.9 * (R2 - R1) * wallt ** 0.9
    behind = np.clip(r - R2, 0, None)
    H = (h_apron + h_wall - 0.7 * behind) * open_
    H -= 30 * g * wallt * open_                                     # gullies cut the wall
    # cliff bands: terrace the headwall
    band = 24.0
    Ht = np.floor(H / band) * band + band * np.clip((H / band % 1 - 0.3) / 0.7, 0, 1) ** 1.5
    wk = np.clip(wallt * 4, 0, 1) * np.clip((1 - wallt) * 6, 0, 1) * open_
    H = H * (1 - 0.7 * wk) + Ht * 0.7 * wk
    # floor basin and regional tilt (the valley falls toward the camera)
    H -= 14 * np.exp(-((dx / 110) ** 2 + ((dy + 40) / 80) ** 2))
    H += 0.08 * (Y - cy) * (1 - open_) + 0.05 * Y
    H += 16 * LF.eroded_fbm(X, Y, 260, octaves=6, seed=seed) * (0.3 + wallt) + 2 * LF.fbm(X, Y, 25, seed=seed + 5)
    if erode:
        H = LF.droplet_erosion(H, cell, n_drops=120000, seed=seed)
    T = Terrain(H, cell)
    slope = T.slope_deg()
    conc = T.concavity(20)
    lvl = np.percentile(H[r &lt; 120], 20)
    lab, n = ndi.label(H &lt; lvl)
    ci, cj = int(cx / cell), int((cy - 40) / cell)
    tarn = (lab == lab[cj, ci]) if lab[cj, ci] else np.zeros_like(H, bool)
    level = np.where(tarn, lvl, np.nan)
    rough = LF.fbm(X, Y, 50, seed=seed + 6)
    cliff = ((slope &gt; 40) | ((wk &gt; 0.3) &amp; (slope &gt; 30))).astype(float)
    ledge = (wk &gt; 0.3) &amp; (slope &lt; 30)
    scree = ((apron &gt; 0.05) &amp; (apron &lt; 1.0) &amp; (r &lt; R1 + 5) &amp; (slope &gt; 12)).astype(float) * open_
    meadow = np.clip(1 - slope / 28, 0, 1) * 0.7
    snow_w = snow * 1.2 + 0.9 * g * np.clip(wallt * 2, 0, 1) + 0.6 * ledge + 0.5 * np.clip((apron - 0.7) * 3, 0, 1) * g \\
        + 0.25 * rough - 0.9 + 0.3 * np.clip(behind / 60, 0, 1)
    snow_m = (snow_w &gt; 0.3) &amp; (slope &lt; 50)
    cover = dict(meadow=meadow, scree=scree * 0.95, rock=cliff * 1.1 + 0.3 * wallt + 0.2 * (behind &gt; 0),
                 snow=snow_m.astype(float) * 1.5)
    forest = ((slope &lt; 22) &amp; (r &gt; 300) &amp; (open_ &lt; 0.6) &amp; (rough &gt; -0.05) &amp; (Y &lt; cy)).astype(float)
    cover[&quot;forest&quot;] = forest
    tr = TC.scatter_trees(T, forest * 0.85, 6.0, rng, hmean=16) if trees else None
    extras = dict(cliff=cliff, conc=conc, apron=apron * (r &lt; R1 + 5), gully=g, ledge=ledge.astype(float))
    return Scene(T, cover, trees=tr, water=dict(level=level, flow=0.0), extras=extras, seed=seed)
&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;
open(p,&#x27;w&#x27;).write(s)

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Write: aerial/lib/tech_rock.py</code></summary><pre>&quot;&quot;&quot;tech_rock.py -- mountain ground from above: scree fans, cliff bands, snow fields.

SCREE FAN (photos: Isfjorden talus cones, Pic de Jallouvre / Lac de Lessy, Watson River cirque;
Ferrari V19 blockfields)
  * a fan is sorted: fine at the apex, coarse at the toe (the biggest blocks roll furthest);
  * from above, texture is the only thing that says &#x27;scree&#x27;: grain size in pixels grows down the fan;
  * it is striped along the fall line by debris channels and levees (darker lines radiating from
    the gully mouth);
  * individual blocks, once &gt;= ~2 px, are drawn as Ferrari&#x27;s stones: a light top row, a dark bottom
    row (the contrast is inside the stone; the mass value between stones stays flat).
CLIFF BANDS (photos: Isfjorden, Cloud Peak/Glacier NP; Ferrari V14, V19)
  * a banded cliff from above is a stack of risers (planes, lit or shaded as one value) and ledges;
  * each ledge is a 1-px light line (lit lip, often snow- or grass-covered) with a dark line under it
    (the overhang / the riser in its own shadow) -- found against the light, lost in the dark;
  * gullies cut the bands as dark fall-line slots.
SNOW (photos: North Cascades B&amp;W, Neve glacier, Watson River, Marcus Merlon)
  * lit snow is nearly flat (one value, maybe two); all the drawing is in the SHADOWED HOLLOWS:
    smooth blue (sky-lit) shapes with a hard edge on the sun side and a soft reflected-light side;
  * the snow edge against rock is crisp; its downhill edge carries a 1-px darker lip (thickness);
  * near its edges rocks poke through as isolated dark pixels.
&quot;&quot;&quot;
import numpy as np
from dither import threshold_map
from noise import voronoi, cell_rand


def paint_scree(cv, mask, G, apron, sunlit, ndl, cam, seed=0, band=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;apron: 0 at the toe .. 1 at the apex (per pixel).  Scree ramp (5): 0 gap | 1 shadow || 2 half | 3 lit | 4 top.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    h, w = mask.shape
    if not mask.any():
        return
    mpp = G[&quot;mpp&quot;]
    size = 0.12 + 2.4 * np.clip(1 - apron, 0, 1) ** 1.6          # clast size (m)
    px = size / np.maximum(mpp, 1e-3)
    thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed)
    base = np.where(sunlit, np.where(ndl &gt; 0.45, 3, 2), 1)
    s = base.copy()
    # blocks (&gt;= 1.6 px): world-space cells at three size classes
    x, y = G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;]
    for lo, hi, sc in ((1.6, 3.5, 1.0), (3.5, 7.0, 1.0), (7.0, 99, 1.0)):
        sel = mask &amp; (px &gt;= lo) &amp; (px &lt; hi)
        if not sel.any():
            continue
        cs = float(np.median(size[sel]))
        F1, F2, cid, cx, cy = voronoi(x[sel], y[sel], seed=seed + int(lo * 10), scale=cs)
        edge = (F2 - F1) &lt; 0.18 * cs                       # the gap between stones
        rel_y = (y[sel] - cy) / cs                          # +: far (upper) part of the stone on screen
        top = rel_y &gt; 0.12
        bot = rel_y &lt; -0.22
        v = cell_rand(cid, 1)
        lit = sunlit[sel]
        st = np.where(lit, 2 + (v &gt; 0.35), 1 - (v &gt; 0.8))
        st = np.where(top &amp; lit, np.minimum(st + 1, 4), st)
        st = np.where(bot, np.maximum(st - 1 - lit, 0), st)
        st = np.where(edge, 0, st)
        tmp = s[sel]; tmp[:] = st; s[sel] = tmp
    # grain (&lt; 1.6 px): stipple whose density grows with clast size, plus fall-line channels
    fine = mask &amp; (px &lt; 1.6)
    dens = np.clip(px * 0.22, 0.04, 0.35)
    s = np.where(fine &amp; (thr &lt; dens * 0.5), np.maximum(base - 1, 0), s)
    s = np.where(fine &amp; (thr &gt; 1 - dens * 0.5) &amp; sunlit, np.minimum(base + 1, 4), s)
    # debris channels: thin darker streaks along the fall line (world-space stripes across the fan)
    n = G[&quot;n&quot;]
    ang = np.arctan2(n[..., 1], n[..., 0])                 # downslope azimuth
    stripe = np.sin((x * np.sin(ang) - y * np.cos(ang)) / 3.0 + 3 * np.sin(y / 23.0 + x / 31.0))
    ch = mask &amp; (stripe &gt; 0.93) &amp; (apron &gt; 0.15)
    s = np.where(ch, np.maximum(s - 1, 0), s)
    cv.fill(mask, &quot;scree&quot;, s, band=band)


def paint_cliffs(cv, rockmask, G, ledge, snowy, cam, band=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;ledge lines on a banded cliff: a lit lip on each ledge pixel row and a dark line under it.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    h, w = rockmask.shape
    L = rockmask &amp; ledge
    below = np.zeros_like(L); below[1:] = L[:-1]           # the pixel just under a ledge pixel
    top_of_riser = rockmask &amp; below &amp; ~L
    st = cv.step.copy()
    # lip: rock top step, or snow if the ledge is snowy
    lip = L &amp; ~snowy
    cv.step[lip] = np.clip(st[lip] + 1, 0, 5)
    cv.step[top_of_riser] = 0
    sn = L &amp; snowy
    if sn.any():
        cv.fill(sn, &quot;snow&quot;, 4, band=band)


def paint_snow(cv, mask, G, sunlit, ndl, conc, seed=0, band=None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;snow ramp (6): 0 crevasse/contact | 1 hollow core | 2 reflected || 3 half | 4 lit | 5 glare.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    h, w = mask.shape
    thr = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;bayer4&quot;)
    # lit snow: two flat values only (lit / glare on sun-facing)
    s = np.where(ndl &gt; 0.55, 5, 4)
    s = np.where(ndl &lt; 0.2, 3, s)
    # shadowed hollows: core near the terminator, reflected light deeper in (from the lit snow opposite)
    sh = ~sunlit
    core = sh &amp; (ndl &gt; -0.25)                               # just past the terminator
    s = np.where(sh, np.where(core, 1, 2), s)
    # hollows that the sun still reaches but are concave: a half step down (the shape of the hollow)
    s = np.where(~sh &amp; (conc &lt; -1.5) &amp; (s &gt;= 4) &amp; (thr &lt; 0.5), 3, s)
    # edges: crisp; downhill lip darker; rocks poking through near edges
    lower = np.zeros_like(mask); lower[:-1] = mask[1:]
    lip = mask &amp; ~lower
    s = np.where(lip, np.where(sunlit, 3, 1), s)
    edge_zone = mask &amp; ~(np_erode(mask, 2))
    tb = threshold_map(h, w, &quot;blue&quot;, seed + 9)
    poke = edge_zone &amp; (tb &lt; 0.12)
    cv.fill(mask, &quot;snow&quot;, s, band=band)
    cv.fill(poke, &quot;rock&quot;, np.where(sunlit, 2, 0)[poke] if False else 1, band=band)


def np_erode(m, it):
    from scipy import ndimage as ndi
    return ndi.binary_erosion(m, iterations=it)
</pre></details><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Hook scree, cliff, snow techniques into painter</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;

open(p,&#x27;w&#x27;).write(s)

        G[&quot;mask_&quot; + name] = m
    # ---- mountain techniques on top of the plain slope tone
    import tech_rock as TR
    lit_all = (ndl &gt; 0.06) &amp; (sunvis &gt; 0.5)
    ex = sc.extras
    if &quot;mask_scree&quot; in G:
        apr = T0.sample(ex[&quot;apron&quot;], G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;]) if &quot;apron&quot; in ex else np.full((h, w), 0.5)
        TR.paint_scree(cv, G[&quot;mask_scree&quot;], G, apr, lit_all, ndl, cam, seed=seed, band=band)

        TR.paint_cliffs(cv, G[&quot;mask_rock&quot;], G, led, snowy, cam, band=band)
    if &quot;mask_snow&quot; in G:
        cc_s = T0.sample(ex[&quot;conc&quot;], G[&quot;x&quot;], G[&quot;y&quot;]) if &quot;conc&quot; in ex else np.zeros((h, w))
        TR.paint_snow(cv, G[&quot;mask_snow&quot;], G, lit_all, ndl, cc_s, seed=seed, band=band)

<div class="steps"><details class="step"><summary><code>Bash: Cirque: less erosion, ledge phase, scree priority</code></summary><pre>cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/aerial/lib &amp;&amp; python3 - &lt;&lt;&#x27;EOF&#x27;

    phase = (H / band) % 1.0
    ledge0 = (wk &gt; 0.35) &amp; (phase &lt; 0.22)&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;)

            &quot;cliff = ((slope &gt; 44) | ((wk &gt; 0.3) &amp; (slope &gt; 32))).astype(float)\n    ledge = ledge0&quot;)

            &quot;scree = ((apron &gt; 0.02) &amp; (r &lt; R1 + 5) &amp; (slope &gt; 10) &amp; (slope &lt; 48)).astype(float) * np.clip(open_ * 1.5, 0, 1) * 1.4&quot;)
